tests/test_bug14_go_probe.py:174: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

spec = VendorSpec(name='opencode', binary='/Users/nightskat/.opencode/bin/opencode', auth_hint='kiểm tra credentials Go tại ~... api_key_env=None, models_cmd=['opencode', 'models', 'opencode-go'], provider='opencode-go', quota_group='opencode-go')
which = <function fake_which.<locals>.<lambda> at 0x10a56c860>
runner = <function fake_runner_ok at 0x108a09940>
records = [{'model': 'opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash', 'provider': 'opencode-go', 'quota_group': 'opencode-go', 'status': 'ok', ...}]
now = '2026-10-22T12:00:00+00:00'

    def detect(
        spec: VendorSpec,
        which=shutil.which,
        runner=subprocess.run,
        records: list[dict] | None = None,
        now: str | datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VendorProbe:
        if spec.binary is None:
            return _detect_api_key_vendor(spec)
        path_str = which(spec.binary)
        if path_str is None and spec.binary and "/" in spec.binary:
            # Hỗ trợ mock which chỉ so khớp basename thay vì đường dẫn tuyệt đối
            path_str = which(Path(spec.binary).name)
        if path_str is None:
            return VendorProbe(
                name=spec.name,
                path=None,
                authed=False,
                quota_capped=False,
                version=None,
                models=[],
                error=None
            )
    
        abs_path = str(Path(path_str).resolve())
        version = None
        authed: bool | None = False
        error = None
        quota_capped = False
        models: list[str] = []
    
        try:
            if spec.version_cmd:
                cmd = [abs_path] + spec.version_cmd[1:]
                res = runner(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                # Chỉ nhận version khi lệnh thành công — tránh dùng stderr lỗi làm version.
                if res.returncode == 0:
                    version = res.stdout.strip() or None
    
            if spec.auth_check_cmd:
                res = _run_auth_check(spec, abs_path, runner)
                authed = (res.returncode == 0)
                # Codex review: auth-check rớt vì HẾT QUOTA không phải là "chưa auth".
                # Credential vẫn sống → authed=True + quota_capped để máy trạng thái
                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình
            # (assume_authed / auth_check_cmd): bằng chứng lane Go không được đổi trạng thái
            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.
            # Regression BUG-14 lô 5a: dispatcher gọi detect(spec) KHÔNG truyền `now`; nhánh
            # quota cap cũ gọi now.isoformat() trên None → AttributeError, CLI mất luôn JSON.
            if _is_go_lane(spec):
                if records is None:
                    records = _read_evidence_safe()
>               now_iso = normalize_now_iso(now)
                          ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E               NameError: name 'normalize_now_iso' is not defined

        now: str | datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VendorProbe:
        if spec.binary is None:
            return _detect_api_key_vendor(spec)
        path_str = which(spec.binary)
        if path_str is None and spec.binary and "/" in spec.binary:
            # Hỗ trợ mock which chỉ so khớp basename thay vì đường dẫn tuyệt đối
            path_str = which(Path(spec.binary).name)
        if path_str is None:
            return VendorProbe(
                name=spec.name,
                path=None,
                authed=False,
                quota_capped=False,
                version=None,
                models=[],
                error=None
            )
    
        abs_path = str(Path(path_str).resolve())
        version = None
        authed: bool | None = False
        error = None
        quota_capped = False
        models: list[str] = []
    
        try:
            if spec.version_cmd:
                cmd = [abs_path] + spec.version_cmd[1:]
                res = runner(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                # Chỉ nhận version khi lệnh thành công — tránh dùng stderr lỗi làm version.
                if res.returncode == 0:
                    version = res.stdout.strip() or None
    
            if spec.auth_check_cmd:
                res = _run_auth_check(spec, abs_path, runner)
                authed = (res.returncode == 0)
                # Codex review: auth-check rớt vì HẾT QUOTA không phải là "chưa auth".
                # Credential vẫn sống → authed=True + quota_capped để máy trạng thái
                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình
            # (assume_authed / auth_check_cmd): bằng chứng lane Go không được đổi trạng thái
            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.
            # Regression BUG-14 lô 5a: dispatcher gọi detect(spec) KHÔNG truyền `now`; nhánh
            # quota cap cũ gọi now.isoformat() trên None → AttributeError, CLI mất luôn JSON.
            if _is_go_lane(spec):
                if records is None:
                    records = _read_evidence_safe()

                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình
            # (assume_authed / auth_check_cmd): bằng chứng lane Go không được đổi trạng thái
            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.
            # Regression BUG-14 lô 5a: dispatcher gọi detect(spec) KHÔNG truyền `now`; nhánh
            # quota cap cũ gọi now.isoformat() trên None → AttributeError, CLI mất luôn JSON.
            if _is_go_lane(spec):
                if records is None:
                    records = _read_evidence_safe()

    def detect(
        spec: VendorSpec,
        which=shutil.which,
        runner=subprocess.run,
        records: list[dict] | None = None,
        now: str | datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VendorProbe:
        if spec.binary is None:
            return _detect_api_key_vendor(spec)
        path_str = which(spec.binary)
        if path_str is None and spec.binary and "/" in spec.binary:
            # Hỗ trợ mock which chỉ so khớp basename thay vì đường dẫn tuyệt đối
            path_str = which(Path(spec.binary).name)
        if path_str is None:
            return VendorProbe(
                name=spec.name,
                path=None,
                authed=False,
                quota_capped=False,
                version=None,
                models=[],
                error=None
            )

        version = None
        authed: bool | None = False
        error = None
        quota_capped = False

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

    def _reject_shadow_repo(workdir: str | None, vendor: str | None) -> None:
        """Chặn ``--cd`` trỏ vào một cây KHÁC có synthetic manifest riêng.
    
        Artifact synthetic luôn đọc từ ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` nên workdir không mở được
        cổng; nhưng một "repo giả" (copy cây repo + manifest tự viết) là dấu hiệu
        caller đang cố lái cổng sang dữ liệu khác ⇒ chặn trước khi chạm file.
        Workdir là repo thật (hoặc con của nó) vẫn hợp lệ; workdir thường cũng hợp lệ.
        """
        if not workdir:
            return
        try:
            from pathlib import Path
            from lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        except ImportError:
            from pathlib import Path
            from bin.lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        try:
            wd = Path(workdir).resolve(strict=False)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
            return
        real = Path(REAL_REPO_ROOT).resolve(strict=False)
        if wd == real or real in wd.parents:
            return
        if (wd / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic" / "manifest.json").is_file():
            raise _blocked_vision(
                vendor, "workdir trỏ vào cây khác có synthetic manifest (repo giả)"
            )
    
    
    def _assert_model_accepts_vision(
        model: str | None, attachments: list[str], vendor: str | None
    ) -> None:
        """Model phải là model đã biết VÀ có khả năng nhận input ảnh/PDF.
    
>       Chạy trước synthetic gate để một model không nhận vision (hoặc slug lạ
        ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       NameError: name '_reject_shadow_repo' is not defined

spec = VendorSpec(name='opencode', binary='/Users/nightskat/.opencode/bin/opencode', auth_hint='kiểm tra credentials Go tại ~... api_key_env=None, models_cmd=['opencode', 'models', 'opencode-go'], provider='opencode-go', quota_group='opencode-go')
which = <function test_installed_opencode_is_auth_unverified_not_not_authed_or_ready.<locals>.<lambda> at 0x10a5c0900>
runner = <function test_installed_opencode_is_auth_unverified_not_not_authed_or_ready.<locals>.runner at 0x10a5c02c0>
records = [{'latency_ms': 0, 'model': 'grok-4.6', 'reason': None, 'served_model': None, ...}, {'latency_ms': 0, 'model': 'grok-4.6', 'reason': None, 'served_model': None, ...}]
now = None

    def detect(
        spec: VendorSpec,
        which=shutil.which,
        runner=subprocess.run,
        records: list[dict] | None = None,
        now: str | datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VendorProbe:
        if spec.binary is None:
            return _detect_api_key_vendor(spec)
        path_str = which(spec.binary)
        if path_str is None and spec.binary and "/" in spec.binary:
            # Hỗ trợ mock which chỉ so khớp basename thay vì đường dẫn tuyệt đối
            path_str = which(Path(spec.binary).name)
        if path_str is None:
            return VendorProbe(
                name=spec.name,
                path=None,
                authed=False,
                quota_capped=False,
                version=None,
                models=[],
                error=None
            )
    
        abs_path = str(Path(path_str).resolve())
        version = None
        authed: bool | None = False
        error = None
        quota_capped = False
        models: list[str] = []
    
        try:
            if spec.version_cmd:
                cmd = [abs_path] + spec.version_cmd[1:]
                res = runner(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                # Chỉ nhận version khi lệnh thành công — tránh dùng stderr lỗi làm version.
                if res.returncode == 0:
                    version = res.stdout.strip() or None
    
            if spec.auth_check_cmd:
                res = _run_auth_check(spec, abs_path, runner)
                authed = (res.returncode == 0)
                # Codex review: auth-check rớt vì HẾT QUOTA không phải là "chưa auth".
                # Credential vẫn sống → authed=True + quota_capped để máy trạng thái
                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình
            # (assume_authed / auth_check_cmd): bằng chứng lane Go không được đổi trạng thái
            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.
            # Regression BUG-14 lô 5a: dispatcher gọi detect(spec) KHÔNG truyền `now`; nhánh
            # quota cap cũ gọi now.isoformat() trên None → AttributeError, CLI mất luôn JSON.
            if _is_go_lane(spec):
                if records is None:
                    records = _read_evidence_safe()
>               now_iso = normalize_now_iso(now)
                          ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E               NameError: name 'normalize_now_iso' is not defined

                # Credential vẫn sống → authed=True + quota_capped để máy trạng thái
                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình
            # (assume_authed / auth_check_cmd): bằng chứng lane Go không được đổi trạng thái
            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.
            # Regression BUG-14 lô 5a: dispatcher gọi detect(spec) KHÔNG truyền `now`; nhánh
            # quota cap cũ gọi now.isoformat() trên None → AttributeError, CLI mất luôn JSON.
            if _is_go_lane(spec):
                if records is None:
                    records = _read_evidence_safe()
>               now_iso = normalize_now_iso(now)
                          ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E               NameError: name 'normalize_now_iso' is not defined

    def test_opencode_does_not_infer_ready_from_catalog():
        """Catalog có 28 model cũng KHÔNG được suy thành ready."""
        catalog_output = "\n".join([
            "opencode-go/deepseek-v4.1-flash",
            "opencode-go/glm-5.3-flash",
            "opencode-go/mimo-v2.6-flash",
        ])
        def runner(cmd, **kw):
            if "--version" in cmd:
                return FakeCompleted(returncode=0, stdout="opencode 1.0.0")
            if "models" in cmd:
                return FakeCompleted(returncode=0, stdout=catalog_output)
            return FakeCompleted(returncode=0)

    def detect(
        spec: VendorSpec,
        which=shutil.which,
        runner=subprocess.run,
        records: list[dict] | None = None,
        now: str | datetime | None = None,
    ) -> VendorProbe:
        if spec.binary is None:
            return _detect_api_key_vendor(spec)
        path_str = which(spec.binary)
        if path_str is None and spec.binary and "/" in spec.binary:
            # Hỗ trợ mock which chỉ so khớp basename thay vì đường dẫn tuyệt đối
            path_str = which(Path(spec.binary).name)
        if path_str is None:
            return VendorProbe(
                name=spec.name,
                path=None,
                authed=False,
                quota_capped=False,
                version=None,
                models=[],
                error=None
            )
    
        abs_path = str(Path(path_str).resolve())
        version = None
        authed: bool | None = False
        error = None
        quota_capped = False
        models: list[str] = []
    
        try:
            if spec.version_cmd:
                cmd = [abs_path] + spec.version_cmd[1:]
                res = runner(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                # Chỉ nhận version khi lệnh thành công — tránh dùng stderr lỗi làm version.
                if res.returncode == 0:
                    version = res.stdout.strip() or None
    
            if spec.auth_check_cmd:
                res = _run_auth_check(spec, abs_path, runner)
                authed = (res.returncode == 0)
                # Codex review: auth-check rớt vì HẾT QUOTA không phải là "chưa auth".
                # Credential vẫn sống → authed=True + quota_capped để máy trạng thái
                # ra `quota_capped`, đúng lane failover thay vì bảo user đi login lại.
                if not authed and is_quota_error(res.stderr or "", res.returncode):
                    authed, quota_capped = True, True
                    error = error or f"quota (exit {res.returncode})"
                elif (not authed and not spec.auth_check_authoritative
                      and not _AUTH_FAILURE_RE.search(res.stderr or "")):
                    authed = None
                    # BUG-13 (20/08): trước đây chỉ ghi mã thoát, VỨT stderr. Người
                    # đọc thấy nhãn `auth_unverified` sẽ đi kiểm auth — trong khi
                    # nguyên nhân thật có thể là cờ sai, mạng, hay CLI đổi cú pháp.
                    # Nhãn nói SAI CHỖ, mà bằng chứng để cãi lại thì đã bị vứt.
                    chi_tiet = " | ".join((res.stderr or "").strip().splitlines()[-3:])[:300]
                    error = f"auth_probe_unverified (exit {res.returncode})"
                    if chi_tiet:
                        error += f" — stderr: {chi_tiet}"
            else:
                # Không có cách kiểm auth (auth_check_cmd=None):
                # assume_authed=True -> authed=True (host claude)
                # assume_authed=None -> authed=None (auth_unverified: không có verified auth cmd, không dump credential, không inference)
                # assume_authed=False -> authed=False (installed_not_authed)
                authed = spec.assume_authed
    
            # BUG-14: suy auth/quota từ live dispatch evidence — CHỈ lane Go và CHỈ khi có
            # mốc thời gian để so TTL. Vendor khác giữ nguyên kết luận của probe mình

            # claude/codex/agy/gemini, và evidence không có `ts` thì không phải bằng chứng.

_______________ test_transport_timeout_in_detect_preserves_error _______________

    def test_transport_timeout_in_detect_preserves_error():
        # 1. Version command timeout
        def runner_version_timeout(cmd, **kw):
            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=cmd, timeout=10)
    
        p_hermes = detect(REGISTRY["hermes"], which=lambda b: "/bin/hermes", runner=runner_version_timeout)
        assert p_hermes.error is not None
        assert "timeout" in p_hermes.error.lower()
    
        # 2. Models command timeout (opencode)
        def runner_models_timeout(cmd, **kw):
            if "--version" in cmd:
                return FakeCompleted(returncode=0, stdout="opencode 1.0.0")
            raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired(cmd=cmd, timeout=10)
    
>       p_opencode = detect(
            REGISTRY["opencode"],
            which=lambda b: "/fake/home/.opencode/bin/opencode",
            runner=runner_models_timeout,
        )

tests/test_go_nopii_gate.py:166: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

    def _reject_shadow_repo(workdir: str | None, vendor: str | None) -> None:
        """Chặn ``--cd`` trỏ vào một cây KHÁC có synthetic manifest riêng.
    
        Artifact synthetic luôn đọc từ ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` nên workdir không mở được
        cổng; nhưng một "repo giả" (copy cây repo + manifest tự viết) là dấu hiệu
        caller đang cố lái cổng sang dữ liệu khác ⇒ chặn trước khi chạm file.
        Workdir là repo thật (hoặc con của nó) vẫn hợp lệ; workdir thường cũng hợp lệ.
        """
        if not workdir:
            return
        try:
            from pathlib import Path
            from lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        except ImportError:
            from pathlib import Path
            from bin.lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        try:
            wd = Path(workdir).resolve(strict=False)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
            return
        real = Path(REAL_REPO_ROOT).resolve(strict=False)
        if wd == real or real in wd.parents:
            return
        if (wd / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic" / "manifest.json").is_file():
            raise _blocked_vision(
                vendor, "workdir trỏ vào cây khác có synthetic manifest (repo giả)"
            )
    
    
    def _assert_model_accepts_vision(
        model: str | None, attachments: list[str], vendor: str | None
    ) -> None:
        """Model phải là model đã biết VÀ có khả năng nhận input ảnh/PDF.
    
>       Chạy trước synthetic gate để một model không nhận vision (hoặc slug lạ
        ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       NameError: name '_reject_shadow_repo' is not defined

        ]

                    data_class=DATA_CLASS_CODE_NOPII, prompt="code", vendor="hermes", **kw
                )

    def _reject_shadow_repo(workdir: str | None, vendor: str | None) -> None:

        """Chặn ``--cd`` trỏ vào một cây KHÁC có synthetic manifest riêng.
    
        Artifact synthetic luôn đọc từ ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` nên workdir không mở được
        cổng; nhưng một "repo giả" (copy cây repo + manifest tự viết) là dấu hiệu
        caller đang cố lái cổng sang dữ liệu khác ⇒ chặn trước khi chạm file.
        Workdir là repo thật (hoặc con của nó) vẫn hợp lệ; workdir thường cũng hợp lệ.
        """
        if not workdir:
            return
        try:
            from pathlib import Path
            from lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        except ImportError:
            from pathlib import Path
            from bin.lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        try:
            wd = Path(workdir).resolve(strict=False)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
            return
        real = Path(REAL_REPO_ROOT).resolve(strict=False)
        if wd == real or real in wd.parents:
            return
        if (wd / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic" / "manifest.json").is_file():
            raise _blocked_vision(
                vendor, "workdir trỏ vào cây khác có synthetic manifest (repo giả)"
            )
    
    
    def _assert_model_accepts_vision(
        model: str | None, attachments: list[str], vendor: str | None
    ) -> None:
        """Model phải là model đã biết VÀ có khả năng nhận input ảnh/PDF.

    def _reject_shadow_repo(workdir: str | None, vendor: str | None) -> None:
        """Chặn ``--cd`` trỏ vào một cây KHÁC có synthetic manifest riêng.
    
        Artifact synthetic luôn đọc từ ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` nên workdir không mở được
        cổng; nhưng một "repo giả" (copy cây repo + manifest tự viết) là dấu hiệu
        caller đang cố lái cổng sang dữ liệu khác ⇒ chặn trước khi chạm file.
        Workdir là repo thật (hoặc con của nó) vẫn hợp lệ; workdir thường cũng hợp lệ.
        """
        if not workdir:
            return
        try:
            from pathlib import Path
            from lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        except ImportError:
            from pathlib import Path
            from bin.lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        try:
            wd = Path(workdir).resolve(strict=False)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
            return
        real = Path(REAL_REPO_ROOT).resolve(strict=False)
        if wd == real or real in wd.parents:
            return
        if (wd / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic" / "manifest.json").is_file():
            raise _blocked_vision(
                vendor, "workdir trỏ vào cây khác có synthetic manifest (repo giả)"
            )
    
    
    def _assert_model_accepts_vision(
        model: str | None, attachments: list[str], vendor: str | None
    ) -> None:
        """Model phải là model đã biết VÀ có khả năng nhận input ảnh/PDF.

    def _reject_shadow_repo(workdir: str | None, vendor: str | None) -> None:
        """Chặn ``--cd`` trỏ vào một cây KHÁC có synthetic manifest riêng.
    
        Artifact synthetic luôn đọc từ ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` nên workdir không mở được
        cổng; nhưng một "repo giả" (copy cây repo + manifest tự viết) là dấu hiệu
        caller đang cố lái cổng sang dữ liệu khác ⇒ chặn trước khi chạm file.
        Workdir là repo thật (hoặc con của nó) vẫn hợp lệ; workdir thường cũng hợp lệ.
        """

            return

            from lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        except ImportError:
            from pathlib import Path
            from bin.lib.vision import REAL_REPO_ROOT
        try:
            wd = Path(workdir).resolve(strict=False)
        except (OSError, RuntimeError):
            return
        real = Path(REAL_REPO_ROOT).resolve(strict=False)
        if wd == real or real in wd.parents:
            return

                vendor, "workdir trỏ vào cây khác có synthetic manifest (repo giả)"

    def check_vision_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        model: str,
        provider: str = "",
        adapter: str = "",
        is_synthetic: bool = False,
        allow_sensitive: bool = False,
        confirmation: Any = None,
        is_retry: bool = False,
        is_failover: bool = False,
        workspace_root: str | Path | None = None,
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Pure gate check before ANY file I/O, subprocess, or network call.
    
        GATE CHECK ORDER:
        1. Sensitive / synthetic gate:
           - Dữ liệu thật / sensitive flags luôn bị chặn fail-closed kể cả no files.
           - Chỉ synthetic explicit mới được đi tiếp.
        2. Adapter verification check:
           - Adapter rỗng ("" / None / whitespace) bị chặn lập tức.
           - Chỉ chấp nhận adapter nằm trong frozen VERIFIED_VISION_ADAPTERS registry (opencode).
        3. Capabilities metadata check (theo model cụ thể).
        4. Kiểm tra file sau gate (synthetic generator-backed):
           - Manifest schema_version=1, generator bench/vision/generate.py.
           - Exact SHA-256 match với pinned trusted registry.
           - Không symlink bất kỳ ancestor nào, không traversal/outside.
        5. Kiểm tra tính tương thích giữa model capabilities và MIME đính kèm.
        """
        # Cờ sensitive hoặc confirmation luôn bị chặn dứt khoát
        if allow_sensitive:
>           raise VisionBlockedError(
                "--allow-sensitive-vision is blocked fail-closed; sensitive vision unsupported."
            )
E           bin.lib.vision.VisionBlockedError: --allow-sensitive-vision is blocked fail-closed; sensitive vision unsupported.

    def check_vision_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        model: str,
        provider: str = "",
        adapter: str = "",
        is_synthetic: bool = False,
        allow_sensitive: bool = False,
        confirmation: Any = None,
        is_retry: bool = False,
        is_failover: bool = False,
        workspace_root: str | Path | None = None,
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Pure gate check before ANY file I/O, subprocess, or network call.
    
        GATE CHECK ORDER:
        1. Sensitive / synthetic gate:
           - Dữ liệu thật / sensitive flags luôn bị chặn fail-closed kể cả no files.
           - Chỉ synthetic explicit mới được đi tiếp.
        2. Adapter verification check:
           - Adapter rỗng ("" / None / whitespace) bị chặn lập tức.
           - Chỉ chấp nhận adapter nằm trong frozen VERIFIED_VISION_ADAPTERS registry (opencode).
        3. Capabilities metadata check (theo model cụ thể).
        4. Kiểm tra file sau gate (synthetic generator-backed):
           - Manifest schema_version=1, generator bench/vision/generate.py.
           - Exact SHA-256 match với pinned trusted registry.
           - Không symlink bất kỳ ancestor nào, không traversal/outside.
        5. Kiểm tra tính tương thích giữa model capabilities và MIME đính kèm.
        """
        # Cờ sensitive hoặc confirmation luôn bị chặn dứt khoát
        if allow_sensitive:
>           raise VisionBlockedError(
                "--allow-sensitive-vision is blocked fail-closed; sensitive vision unsupported."
            )

_________ TestSyntheticPathSecurity.test_valid_synthetic_regular_file __________

files = [PosixPath('/private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_valid_synthetic_regular_f0/workspace/test.png')]
max_bytes = 20971520

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):

                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p

            try:

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))
E           ValueError: '/private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_path_outside_workspace_bl0/outside/external.png' is not in the subpath of '/Users/nightskat/Developer/polykit-wt-bench/bench/vision/synthetic' OR one path is relative and the other is absolute.

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
>               raise VisionSecurityError(
                    f"Attachment {item} is outside fixed synthetic directory: {synth_dir}"
                )
E               bin.lib.vision.VisionSecurityError: Attachment /private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_path_with_whitespace_supp0/workspace/my synthetic documents/diagram draft v1.png is outside fixed synthetic directory: /Users/nightskat/Developer/polykit-wt-bench/bench/vision/synthetic

        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):

                )

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

        """

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
>               raise VisionSecurityError(
                    f"Attachment {item} is outside fixed synthetic directory: {synth_dir}"
                )
E               bin.lib.vision.VisionSecurityError: Attachment /private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_png_suffix_with_fake_cont0/workspace/fake.png is outside fixed synthetic directory: /Users/nightskat/Developer/polykit-wt-bench/bench/vision/synthetic

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))
