            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p

            try:

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):

                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
>               raise VisionSecurityError(

                )

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,
            parse_int=None, parse_constant=None, object_pairs_hook=None, **kw):
        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance
        containing a JSON document) to a Python object.
    
        ``object_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).
    
        ``object_pairs_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.  The
        return value of ``object_pairs_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``.
        This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If ``object_hook``
        is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",
                                      s, 0)
        else:
            if not isinstance(s, (bytes, bytearray)):
>               raise TypeError(f'the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, '
                                f'not {s.__class__.__name__}')
E               TypeError: the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, not MagicMock

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )

                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))
E           ValueError: '/private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_test_only_monkeypatched_a0/workspace/img.png' is not in the subpath of '/Users/nightskat/Developer/polykit-wt-bench/bench/vision/synthetic' OR one path is relative and the other is absolute.

        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
                rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                if len(rel.parts) != 1:
                    raise VisionSecurityError(
                        f"Attachment {item} must be directly inside {synth_dir}"
                    )
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
>               raise VisionSecurityError(
                    f"Attachment {item} is outside fixed synthetic directory: {synth_dir}"
                )
E               bin.lib.vision.VisionSecurityError: Attachment /private/var/folders/c4/49lqjv4j6s72pt3k0tn9fcrr0000gn/T/pytest-of-nightskat/pytest-198/test_pdf_rejected_when_model_s0/workspace/doc.pdf is outside fixed synthetic directory: /Users/nightskat/Developer/polykit-wt-bench/bench/vision/synthetic

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"

                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def loads(s, *, cls=None, object_hook=None, parse_float=None,
            parse_int=None, parse_constant=None, object_pairs_hook=None, **kw):
        """Deserialize ``s`` (a ``str``, ``bytes`` or ``bytearray`` instance
        containing a JSON document) to a Python object.
    
        ``object_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decode (a ``dict``). The return value of
        ``object_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``. This feature
        can be used to implement custom decoders (e.g. JSON-RPC class hinting).
    
        ``object_pairs_hook`` is an optional function that will be called with the
        result of any object literal decoded with an ordered list of pairs.  The
        return value of ``object_pairs_hook`` will be used instead of the ``dict``.
        This feature can be used to implement custom decoders.  If ``object_hook``
        is also defined, the ``object_pairs_hook`` takes priority.
    
        ``parse_float``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON float to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        float(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON floats (e.g. decimal.Decimal).
    
        ``parse_int``, if specified, will be called with the string
        of every JSON int to be decoded. By default this is equivalent to
        int(num_str). This can be used to use another datatype or parser
        for JSON integers (e.g. float).
    
        ``parse_constant``, if specified, will be called with one of the
        following strings: -Infinity, Infinity, NaN.
        This can be used to raise an exception if invalid JSON numbers
        are encountered.
    
        To use a custom ``JSONDecoder`` subclass, specify it with the ``cls``
        kwarg; otherwise ``JSONDecoder`` is used.
        """
        if isinstance(s, str):
            if s.startswith('\ufeff'):
                raise JSONDecodeError("Unexpected UTF-8 BOM (decode using utf-8-sig)",
                                      s, 0)
        else:
            if not isinstance(s, (bytes, bytearray)):
>               raise TypeError(f'the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, '
                                f'not {s.__class__.__name__}')
E               TypeError: the JSON object must be str, bytes or bytearray, not MagicMock

    def validate_synthetic_gate(
        files: Sequence[str | Path],
        max_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_FILE_BYTES,
    ) -> list[VisionAttachment]:
        """Strict security and authenticity gate for synthetic vision attachments:
        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")
            p = Path(item)
    
            # Path traversal check
            if ".." in p.parts:
                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")
    
            # Resolve target path relative to synth_dir
            if p.is_absolute():
                target_path = p
            elif len(p.parts) == 1:
                target_path = synth_dir / p.name
            else:
                # e.g. bench/vision/synthetic/page_01.png
                target_path = root / p
    
            # Check outside synthetic dir
            try:
>               rel = target_path.resolve(strict=False).relative_to(synth_dir.resolve(strict=False))
                      ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

        - Fixed bench/vision/synthetic/ under REAL_REPO_ROOT (immune to fake --cd/cwd).
        - Manifest schema_version=1, generator=bench/vision/generate.py.
        - Manifest entries match pinned trusted hashes registry.
        - Manifest and all ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - Attachment files must reside inside bench/vision/synthetic/.
        - Attachment files and all their ancestor directories must NOT be symlinks.
        - No path traversal, no outside repo, no malformed, duplicate, or missing files.
        - Requested filename MUST appear in the manifest (manifest là danh sách đóng:
          file có trong registry nhưng không được generator khai báo vẫn bị chặn).
        - Exact SHA-256 match against manifest, trusted registry, and file on disk.
        - Magic bytes and file size match expected.
    
        Không có tham số ``repo_root``: cổng luôn đọc constant module
        ``REAL_REPO_ROOT`` (monkeypatch được trong test, sản phẩm thì không).
        """
        if not files:
            raise VisionFormatError("No attachment files provided for vision")
    
        root = REAL_REPO_ROOT
        trusted_reg = TRUSTED_SYNTHETIC_REGISTRY
        synth_dir = root / "bench" / "vision" / "synthetic"
        manifest_path = synth_dir / "manifest.json"
    
        # 1. Symlink checks on directory hierarchy and manifest
        check_no_symlinks(synth_dir, root)
        check_no_symlinks(manifest_path, root)
    
        # 2. Check manifest existence & regular file
        if not manifest_path.exists() or not manifest_path.is_file():
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Synthetic manifest not found: {manifest_path}")
    
        # 3. Parse manifest
        try:
            with open(manifest_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                manifest = json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            raise VisionBlockedError(f"Malformed synthetic manifest JSON: {e}") from e
    
        if not isinstance(manifest, dict):
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest root must be a JSON object")
    
        if manifest.get("schema_version") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest schema_version: {manifest.get('schema_version')}"
            )
    
        if manifest.get("generator") != EXPECTED_MANIFEST_GENERATOR:
            raise VisionBlockedError(
                f"Invalid manifest generator: {manifest.get('generator')}"
            )
    
        manifest_files = manifest.get("files")
        if not isinstance(manifest_files, list) or not manifest_files:
            raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest files must be a non-empty list")
    
        # 4. Verify manifest against trusted generator registry (pinned artifact)
        seen_manifest_files: set[str] = set()
        manifest_map: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    
        for entry in manifest_files:
            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file entry must be a dictionary")
            fname = entry.get("path")
            if not fname or not isinstance(fname, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError("Manifest file missing path string")
            if fname in seen_manifest_files:
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Duplicate file in manifest: {fname}")
            seen_manifest_files.add(fname)
    
            if fname not in trusted_reg:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest file {fname} is not in trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            sha = entry.get("sha256")
            if not sha or not isinstance(sha, str):
                raise VisionBlockedError(f"Manifest file {fname} missing sha256")
    
            expected_sha = trusted_reg[fname]["sha256"]
            if sha.lower() != expected_sha.lower():
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest sha256 mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            expected_bytes = trusted_reg[fname].get("bytes")
            if expected_bytes is not None and entry.get("bytes") != expected_bytes:
                raise VisionBlockedError(
                    f"Manifest byte size mismatch for {fname} against trusted generator registry"
                )
    
            manifest_map[fname] = entry
    
        # 5. Validate caller attachments
        validated: list[VisionAttachment] = []
        seen_attachments: set[str] = set()
    
        for item in files:
            if not item:
                raise VisionFormatError("Empty file path provided")

                raise VisionSecurityError(f"Path traversal detected in attachment: {item}")

                target_path = root / p

    def relative_to(self, *other):
        """Return the relative path to another path identified by the passed
        arguments.  If the operation is not possible (because this is not
        a subpath of the other path), raise ValueError.
        """
        # For the purpose of this method, drive and root are considered
        # separate parts, i.e.:
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('c:')  gives Path('/')
        #   Path('c:/').relative_to('/')   raise ValueError
        if not other:
            raise TypeError("need at least one argument")
        parts = self._parts
        drv = self._drv
        root = self._root
        if root:
            abs_parts = [drv, root] + parts[1:]
        else:
            abs_parts = parts
        to_drv, to_root, to_parts = self._parse_args(other)
        if to_root:
            to_abs_parts = [to_drv, to_root] + to_parts[1:]
        else:
            to_abs_parts = to_parts
        n = len(to_abs_parts)
        cf = self._flavour.casefold_parts
        if (root or drv) if n == 0 else cf(abs_parts[:n]) != cf(to_abs_parts):
            formatted = self._format_parsed_parts(to_drv, to_root, to_parts)
>           raise ValueError("{!r} is not in the subpath of {!r}"
                    " OR one path is relative and the other is absolute."
                             .format(str(self), str(formatted)))

    def test_ca_11_ten_vendor_dump_config_voi_model_mac_dinh_exit_0():
        """Kiểm dump-config các vendor: OR auto blocked expected, Gemini chưa default có thể auto, Go default an toàn effort đúng."""

        from lib.vendors import REGISTRY
        cfg = load_vendor_config()
